                completeness_len=round(comp_len, 3),
                completeness_chunk=round(comp_chunk, 3),
                correctness_px=round(corr_px, 3),
                quality=round(q, 3),
                pred_road_px=n_px,
                found=int(sub.found.sum()), missed=int((~sub.found).sum())))
            print(f"  thr {thr:.2f} {subset:18s} comp_len {comp_len:.3f} "
                  f"comp_chunk {comp_chunk:.3f} corr_px {corr_px:.3f} Q {q:.3f}")
        if abs(thr - a.export_thr) < 1e-9:
            export = ch

    df = pd.DataFrame(acc)
    csv = OUT / "road_score_vs_roads_shp_613590_1m.csv"
    if csv.exists():
        old = pd.read_csv(csv)
        df = pd.concat([old[old.model != label], df], ignore_index=True)
    df.to_csv(csv, index=False)

    if export is not None:
        tag = f"thr{a.export_thr:.2f}".replace(".", "p")
        gpkg = OUT / f"road_found_vs_missed_{tag}_613590_1m.gpkg"
        if gpkg.exists():
            gpkg.unlink()
        export.to_file(gpkg, layer="chunks", driver="GPKG")
        ex_missed = export[~export.found]
        if len(ex_missed):
            ex_missed.to_file(gpkg, layer="missed", driver="GPKG")
        print(f"\nwrote {gpkg}")

    cur = df[df.model == label]
    best = cur[cur.subset == "613590_added_r2"].sort_values("quality").tail(1)
    summ = {"model": label, "prob_raster": str(prob_path),
            "tol_m": TOL_M, "cover_frac": COVER_FRAC, "chunk_m": CHUNK_M,
            "reference": "roads.shp src in %s" % (SRC_ALL,),
            "rows": cur.to_dict(orient="records")}
    if len(best):
        summ["best_thr_on_added"] = float(best.threshold.iloc[0])
    js = OUT / "_road_score_vs_roads_shp_613590_1m.json"
    js.write_text(json.dumps(summ, indent=2, default=float))
    print(f"wrote {csv}\nwrote {js}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```
### agu_poster/_make_poster_figures.py
```python
"""Poster figures for the AGU poster, tile 9t. Every model output shown is held-out (out-of-fold).

Writes agu_poster/figures/:
  poster_hero_heldout_9t.png        held-out pit and pad predictions on hillshade, with a hit, a miss and a false alarm
  poster_channels_one_pit_9t.png    the seven input channels around one pit
  poster_signs_gallery_9t.png       three pits, three pads, three road segments on hillshade, hand outlines
  poster_cv_folds_pit_9t.png        the five held-out block sets of the pit split
  poster_pipeline_diagram.png       processing steps
  poster_pr_curves_heldout_9t.png   precision-recall, ranked candidates, IoU 0.3, held-out
  poster_optical_vs_lidar_9t.png    optical and RRIM pair, from the user's RemoteSensing_option2.pptx

Inputs (read only)
  data/9t/derived/05/hillshade_9t_05.tif, features_pit_9t_05.tif, pit_blocks_9t.gpkg, pad_blocks_9t.gpkg
  data/9t/models/pit/unet_cv5/pit_prob_floor_outoffold_9t_05.tif  (uint8, p = v / 254, 255 = no fold)
  data/9t/models/pad/unet_cv5/fold{k}/pad_prob_cvfold{k}_9t_05.tif + pad_cv5_fold_assignment_9t.csv (block -> fold)
  data/9t/models/{pit,pad}/unet_cv5/*_per_fold_9t.csv  (F2 threshold per fold)
  data/9t/results/operating_point/pr_froc_curve_points_ranked_candidates_heldout_cv5_pit_pitsmrf_pad_9t.csv
  qgis/annotations/annotations_proj.gpkg (pit_inside, pit_outside, plat, roads)

Palette (dataviz validate_palette.js, light, --pairs all, 2026-11-09): pit #eb6834, pad #2a78d6, road #1baf7a.
Pit is orange so the small pits stand out on the translucent blue pads (swapped 2026-11-09 at the user request).
Worst CVD pair road/pad dE 9.2 deutan, tritan 9.6; normal floor 24.0; road 2.74:1 contrast (WARN), so every
class is also named by a direct label. No red carries meaning.

Run
    python agu_poster/_make_poster_figures.py
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

import geopandas as gpd
import matplotlib
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import rasterio
from rasterio.features import rasterize
from rasterio.windows import from_bounds
from scipy import ndimage as ndi

matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.patheffects as pe  # noqa: E402
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402
from matplotlib.patches import FancyArrowPatch, FancyBboxPatch, Rectangle  # noqa: E402
from PIL import Image  # noqa: E402

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = HERE.parent
OUT = HERE / "figures"
D05 = ROOT / "data/9t/derived/05"
MOD = ROOT / "data/9t/models"
ANN = ROOT / "qgis/annotations/annotations_proj.gpkg"
PR = ROOT / "data/9t/results/operating_point/pr_froc_curve_points_ranked_candidates_heldout_cv5_pit_pitsmrf_pad_9t.csv"
DECK = ROOT / "RemoteSensing_option2.pptx"

PIT, PAD, ROAD = "#eb6834", "#2a78d6", "#1baf7a"
# Map overlays sit on the red-tinted RRIM, so they use blue, yellow and white, which separate from the teal and brown RRIM.
MPIT, MPAD, MROAD = "#1A3CFF", "#FFE000", "#FFFFFF"
INK, MUTED, HELD, OTHER = "#2B2F36", "#6B6B6B", "#2a78d6", "#D9D9D9"
DPI = 200
plt.rcParams.update({"font.family": "Georgia", "font.size": 32, "axes.edgecolor": MUTED,
                     "axes.labelcolor": INK, "xtick.color": INK, "ytick.color": INK})
HALO = [pe.withStroke(linewidth=6, foreground="white")]


def read(path, bounds, band=1):
    with rasterio.open(path) as r:
        w = from_bounds(*bounds, r.transform)
        return r.read(band, window=w, boundless=True, fill_value=r.nodata if r.nodata is not None else 0)


def grid(bounds, res=0.5):
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = bounds
    return rasterio.transform.from_origin(x0, y1, res, res), (int(round((y1 - y0) / res)), int(round((x1 - x0) / res)))


def f2_thresholds(name):
    t = pd.read_csv(MOD / name / "unet_cv5" / f"{name}_cv5_per_fold_9t.csv")
    return t[t.objective == "f2"].set_index("fold").prob_threshold.to_dict()


def block_fold(name):
    a = pd.read_csv(MOD / name / "unet_cv5" / f"{name}_cv5_fold_assignment_9t.csv").dropna()
    a = a.drop_duplicates("block_id")
    b = gpd.read_file(D05 / f"{name}_blocks_9t.gpkg", layer="blocks")
    return b.merge(a[["block_id", "fold"]], on="block_id")


def oof(bounds):
    """Held-out probability and threshold maps for pits and pads over bounds."""
    tr, shape = grid(bounds)
    v = read(MOD / "pit/unet_cv5/pit_prob_floor_outoffold_9t_05.tif", bounds)
    pit_p = np.where(v == 255, np.nan, v / 254.0)
    pf = block_fold("pit")
    pit_fold = rasterize(((g, int(f)) for g, f in zip(pf.geometry, pf.fold)), shape, transform=tr, fill=-1)
    th = f2_thresholds("pit")
    pit_t = np.vectorize(lambda f: th.get(f, np.nan))(pit_fold)
    df = block_fold("pad")
    pad_fold = rasterize(((g, int(f)) for g, f in zip(df.geometry, df.fold)), shape, transform=tr, fill=-1)
    pad_p = np.full(shape, np.nan)
    for k in range(5):
        m = pad_fold == k
        if m.any():
            pad_p[m] = read(MOD / f"pad/unet_cv5/fold{k}/pad_prob_cvfold{k}_9t_05.tif", bounds)[m]
    th = f2_thresholds("pad")
    pad_t = np.vectorize(lambda f: th.get(f, np.nan))(pad_fold)
    return pit_p >= pit_t, pad_p >= pad_t, (pit_fold >= 0) & (pad_fold >= 0)


def ann(layer):
    return gpd.read_file(ANN, layer=layer).to_crs("EPSG:6346")


def hs(bounds):
    return read(D05 / "hillshade_9t_05.tif", bounds).astype(float)


def show_hs(ax, bounds):
    """Red Relief Image Map (RGB) as the base image."""
    with rasterio.open(D05 / "rrim_openness_9t_05.tif") as r:
        w = from_bounds(*bounds, r.transform)
        a = np.moveaxis(r.read(window=w, boundless=True, fill_value=0), 0, -1)
    ax.imshow(a, extent=(bounds[0], bounds[2], bounds[1], bounds[3]), interpolation="bilinear")


def sci_scalebar(ax, x, y, length, fs=22, nseg=2):
    """Standard map scale bar: alternating black and white blocks with ticks 0 and full length beneath, on a tight white box.
    (x, y) is the lower-left corner of the bar in data units."""
    x0, x1 = ax.get_xlim(); y0, y1 = ax.get_ylim()
    fig = ax.figure
    w_in = ax.get_position().width * fig.get_figwidth()
    h_in = ax.get_position().height * fig.get_figheight()
    ux, uy = (x1 - x0) / w_in / 72, (y1 - y0) / h_in / 72      # data units per point
    bar_h = 7 * fs / 22 * uy * 2.2
    lab = f"{length:g} m"
    half_lab = 0.5 * len(lab) * fs * 0.55 * ux
    pad = 4 * ux
    top = y + bar_h + 3 * uy
    bot = y - 3 * uy - fs * 1.15 * uy - 3 * uy
    left, right = x - 0.5 * fs * 0.55 * ux - pad, x + length + half_lab + pad
    ax.add_patch(Rectangle((left, bot), right - left, top - bot, fc="white", ec="none", alpha=0.88, zorder=5))
    seg = length / nseg
    for i in range(nseg):
        ax.add_patch(Rectangle((x + i * seg, y), seg, bar_h, fc="black" if i % 2 == 0 else "white", ec="black", lw=1.5, zorder=6))
    ax.text(x, y - 3 * uy, "0", ha="center", va="top", fontsize=fs, color="black", zorder=7)
    ax.text(x + length, y - 3 * uy, lab, ha="center", va="top", fontsize=fs, color="black", zorder=7)


def overlay(ax, mask, bounds, color, alpha=0.55):
    rgba = np.zeros(mask.shape + (4,))
    rgba[mask] = matplotlib.colors.to_rgba(color, alpha)
    ax.imshow(rgba, extent=(bounds[0], bounds[2], bounds[1], bounds[3]), interpolation="nearest")


def outline(ax, gdf, bounds, color, lw=3, ls="-"):
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = bounds
    g = gdf.cx[x0:x1, y0:y1]
    if len(g):
        g.boundary.plot(ax=ax, color="white", linewidth=lw + 3)
        g.boundary.plot(ax=ax, color=color, linewidth=lw, linestyle=ls)


def bare(ax, bounds):
    ax.set_xlim(bounds[0], bounds[2]); ax.set_ylim(bounds[1], bounds[3])
    ax.set_xticks([]); ax.set_yticks([])
    for s in ax.spines.values():
        s.set_visible(False)


def scalebar(ax, bounds, length, label):
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = bounds
    w, h = x1 - x0, y1 - y0
    bx, by = x0 + 0.04 * w, y0 + 0.06 * h
    ax.add_patch(Rectangle((bx - 0.01 * w, by - 0.03 * h), length + 0.02 * w, 0.13 * h, color="white", alpha=0.85))
    ax.plot([bx, bx + length], [by, by], color=INK, lw=6, solid_capstyle="butt")
    ax.text(bx + length / 2, by + 0.015 * h, label, ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=26, color=INK)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def pick_window():
    """375 m tall, 750 m wide window on the pit block grid with full held-out coverage and the most features."""
    pb = block_fold("pit").set_index(["ix", "iy"])
    db = block_fold("pad")
    pits, pads = ann("pit_outside"), ann("plat")
    cands = []
    for (ix, iy), r in pb.iterrows():
        if (ix + 1, iy) not in pb.index:
            continue
        x0, y0, _, y1 = r.geometry.bounds
        b = (x0, y0, x0 + 750, y1)
        from shapely.geometry import box
        bb = box(*b)
        if db.geometry.intersection(bb).area.sum() < 0.999 * bb.area:
            continue
        n = len(pits.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]]) + len(pads.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]])
        cands.append((n, b))
    return [b for n, b in sorted(cands, reverse=True)]


def classify(mask, truth, bounds):
    """Connected predicted objects -> hit / false alarm; truth polygons -> found / missed."""
    tr, shape = grid(bounds)
    lab, n = ndi.label(mask)
    tmask = rasterize(((g, 1) for g in truth.geometry), shape, transform=tr, fill=0).astype(bool)
    objs = ndi.find_objects(lab)
    fa = []
    for i, sl in enumerate(objs, 1):
        o = lab[sl] == i
        if o.sum() >= 16 and not (tmask[sl] & o).any():
            cy, cx = ndi.center_of_mass(o)
            fa.append((bounds[0] + (sl[1].start + cx) * 0.5, bounds[3] - (sl[0].start + cy) * 0.5, int(o.sum())))
    hits, miss = [], []
    for g in truth.geometry:
        m = rasterize([(g, 1)], shape, transform=tr, fill=0).astype(bool)
        if not m.any():
            continue
        (hits if (m & mask).any() else miss).append(g)
    return hits, miss, fa


def fig_hero():
    """Classic multi-panel map. (a) one held-out block with coordinate axes, (b-d) 50 m details, framed legend."""
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch
    pits_rim, pads = ann("pit_outside"), ann("plat")
    for b in pick_window()[:25]:
        b = (b[0], b[1] + 31, b[2], b[3] - 32)      # 750 x 312 m, aspect 2.4, fills the wide panel
        pit_m, pad_m, cover = oof(b)
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = b
        m = 30
        hits, miss, fa = classify(pit_m, pits_rim.cx[x0 + m:x1 - m, y0 + m:y1 - m], b)
        fa = [f for f in fa if x0 + m < f[0] < x1 - m and y0 + m < f[1] < y1 - m]
        if hits and miss and fa:
            break
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(27.6, 8.2), dpi=DPI)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0.075, 0.15, 0.62, 0.83])
    show_hs(ax, b)
    overlay(ax, pad_m & cover, b, MPAD, 0.28)
    overlay(ax, pit_m & cover, b, MPIT, 0.75)
    for g, col, ls in ((pads, MPAD, "--"), (pits_rim, MPIT, "-")):
        gg = g.cx[x0:x1, y0:y1]
        if len(gg):
            gg.boundary.plot(ax=ax, color=col, linewidth=2.2, linestyle=ls)
    ax.set_xlim(x0, x1); ax.set_ylim(y0, y1)
    ax.set_xticks(np.arange(np.ceil(x0 / 250) * 250, x1 + 1, 250))
    ax.set_yticks(np.arange(np.ceil(y0 / 100) * 100 + 100, y1 + 1, 100))
    ax.ticklabel_format(useOffset=False, style="plain")
    ax.tick_params(labelsize=28, length=8, width=1.5)
    ax.set_xlabel("Easting (m), EPSG:6346", fontsize=30)
    ax.set_ylabel("Northing (m)", fontsize=30)
    for s in ax.spines.values():
        s.set_linewidth(1.5); s.set_color(INK)
    # standard scale bar and north arrow, drawn on the map
    sci_scalebar(ax, x0 + 255, y1 - 78, 100, fs=26, nseg=2)
    ax.annotate("", xy=(x1 - 30, y1 - 40), xytext=(x1 - 30, y1 - 78),
                arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle="-|>", color="white", lw=3, mutation_scale=28))
    ax.text(x1 - 30, y1 - 6, "N", ha="center", va="top", fontsize=28, color="white", path_effects=[pe.withStroke(linewidth=5, foreground="black")])
    ax.text(0.012, 0.97, "(a)", transform=ax.transAxes, va="top", fontsize=34, color="black", path_effects=HALO)
    # detail windows
    picks = []
    g = max(hits, key=lambda g: g.area); picks.append(("b", g.centroid.x, g.centroid.y))
    g = miss[0]; picks.append(("c", g.centroid.x, g.centroid.y))
    f = max(fa, key=lambda t_: t_[2]); picks.append(("d", f[0], f[1]))
    half = 25
    for i, (lab, cx, cy) in enumerate(picks):
        ib = (cx - half, cy - half, cx + half, cy + half)
        ax.add_patch(Rectangle((ib[0], ib[1]), 2 * half, 2 * half, fill=False, ec="black", lw=2.5))
        ax.text(ib[2] + 6, ib[3], lab, fontsize=32, color="black", va="top", path_effects=HALO)
        side = 0.30
        ia = fig.add_axes([0.715, 0.68 - i * 0.325 + 0.02, side * 8.2 / 27.6, side])
        pm, _, _ = oof(ib)
        show_hs(ia, ib)
        overlay(ia, pm, ib, MPIT, 0.75)
        for gdf, col, ls in ((pads, MPAD, "--"), (pits_rim, MPIT, "-")):
            gg = gdf.cx[ib[0]:ib[2], ib[1]:ib[3]]
            if len(gg):
                gg.boundary.plot(ax=ia, color=col, linewidth=2.5, linestyle=ls)
        ia.set_xlim(ib[0], ib[2]); ia.set_ylim(ib[1], ib[3]); ia.set_xticks([]); ia.set_yticks([])
        for s in ia.spines.values():
            s.set_linewidth(1.5); s.set_color("black")
        ia.text(0.04, 0.96, f"({lab})", transform=ia.transAxes, va="top", fontsize=30, color="black", path_effects=HALO)
        sci_scalebar(ia, ib[0] + 5, ib[1] + 9, 10, fs=18, nseg=2)
    handles = [Patch(fc=MPIT, ec="none", alpha=0.75, label="Pit, model output"),
               Line2D([0], [0], color=MPIT, lw=3, label="Pit, hand-drawn"),
               Patch(fc=MPAD, ec="none", alpha=0.28, label="Pad, model output"),
               Line2D([0], [0], color=MPAD, lw=3, ls="--", label="Pad, hand-drawn")]
    lg = fig.legend(handles=handles, loc="upper left", bbox_to_anchor=(0.815, 0.98), fontsize=26, frameon=True,
                    fancybox=False, edgecolor="black", framealpha=1.0, borderpad=0.8, labelspacing=0.9,
                    handlelength=2.2)
    lg.get_frame().set_linewidth(1.5)
    fig.savefig(OUT / "poster_hero_heldout_9t.png", dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)
    return b, len(hits), len(miss), len(fa)


def fig_channels():
    pits = ann("pit_outside")
    g = pits.iloc[(pits.area - pits.area.median()).abs().argsort().iloc[0]].geometry
    c = g.centroid
    b = (c.x - 20, c.y - 20, c.x + 20, c.y + 20)
    names = ["Relief 25 m", "Relief 5 m", "Slope", "Position", "Openness +", "Openness −", "Roughness"]
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, 7, figsize=(13.47, 2.9), dpi=DPI)
    fig.subplots_adjust(left=0, right=1, top=0.80, bottom=0.0, wspace=0.04)
    for i, (ax, nm) in enumerate(zip(axs, names)):
        a = read(D05 / "features_pit_9t_05.tif", b, band=i + 1).astype(float)
        lo, hi = np.nanpercentile(a, [2, 98])
        ax.imshow(a, cmap="gray", vmin=lo, vmax=hi)
        ax.set_xticks([]); ax.set_yticks([])
        ax.set_title(nm, fontsize=22, color=INK, pad=6)
    fig.savefig(OUT / "poster_channels_one_pit_9t.png", dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)


def fig_gallery():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20261109)
    with rasterio.open(D05 / "hillshade_9t_05.tif") as r:
        tb = r.bounds
    inner = lambda g: g.cx[tb.left + 100:tb.right - 100, tb.bottom + 100:tb.top - 100]  # noqa: E731
    pits, pads, roads = inner(ann("pit_outside")), inner(ann("plat")), inner(ann("roads"))
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(13.47, 4.4), dpi=DPI)
    fig.subplots_adjust(left=0, right=1, top=1, bottom=0, wspace=0.04)
    keep = {"Pit": 0, "Pad": 1, "Road": 1}   # one example per sign, from the same seeded draw as before
    rows = (("Pit", pits, MPIT, 30, "-"), ("Pad", pads, MPAD, 90, "--"), ("Road", roads, MROAD, 90, "-"))
    for r, (lab, gdf, col, half, ls) in enumerate(rows):
        big = gdf[gdf.length > 120] if lab == "Road" else gdf[gdf.area > gdf.area.quantile(0.4)]
        for c, idx in enumerate(rng.choice(len(big), 3, replace=False)):
            if c != keep[lab]:
                continue
            p = big.iloc[idx].geometry
            pt = p.interpolate(0.5, normalized=True) if lab == "Road" else p.centroid
            b = (pt.x - half, pt.y - half, pt.x + half, pt.y + half)
            ax = axs[r]
            show_hs(ax, b)
            if lab == "Road":
                gdf.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]].plot(ax=ax, color="white", lw=8)
                gdf.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]].plot(ax=ax, color=col, lw=4)
            else:
                outline(ax, gdf, b, col, 4, ls)
            bare(ax, b)
            L = 20 if lab == "Pit" else 50
            ax.set_xlim(b[0], b[2]); ax.set_ylim(b[1], b[3])
            sci_scalebar(ax, b[0] + 0.08 * 2 * half, b[1] + 0.13 * 2 * half, L, fs=24)
    fig.savefig(OUT / "poster_signs_gallery_9t.png", dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)


def fig_folds():
    pf = block_fold("pit")
    allb = gpd.read_file(D05 / "pit_blocks_9t.gpkg", layer="blocks")
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, 5, figsize=(13.47, 2.9), dpi=DPI)
    fig.subplots_adjust(left=0, right=1, top=0.72, bottom=0, wspace=0.06)
    for k, ax in enumerate(axs):
        allb.plot(ax=ax, color="white", edgecolor=OTHER, lw=0.8)
        pf[pf.fold != k].plot(ax=ax, color=OTHER, edgecolor="white", lw=0.8)
        pf[pf.fold == k].plot(ax=ax, color=HELD, edgecolor="white", lw=0.8)
        ax.set_axis_off()
        ax.set_title(f"Test {k + 1}", fontsize=26, color=INK, pad=14)
    fig.savefig(OUT / "poster_cv_folds_pit_9t.png", dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)


def fig_pipeline():
    nl = chr(10)
    rows = ([f"Lidar{nl}points", f"Ground{nl}filter", f"Bare earth{nl}0.5 m", f"7 terrain{nl}layers"],
            [f"U-Net{nl}per sign", "Cutoff", f"Candidate{nl}outlines"])
    W, H = 13.47, 3.6
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(W, H), dpi=DPI)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1]); ax.set_xlim(0, W); ax.set_ylim(0, H); ax.set_axis_off()
    w, h, gap = 2.95, 1.3, 0.47
    prev = None
    for r, (row, y) in enumerate(zip(rows, (2.2, 0.1))):
        for i, s in enumerate(row):
            x = 0.05 + (i if r == 0 else 3 - i) * (w + gap)
            hi = s.startswith("U-Net")  # the model step is the one shaded box
            ax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch((x, y), w, h, boxstyle="square,pad=0.0",
                                        fc="white", ec=INK, lw=2.5))
            ax.text(x + w / 2, y + h / 2, s, ha="center",
                    va="center", fontsize=32, color=INK, fontweight="normal", linespacing=1.0)
            if prev is not None:
                px, py = prev
                if py == y and x > px:
                    a, bb = (px + w + 0.04, y + h / 2), (x - 0.04, y + h / 2)
                elif py == y:
                    a, bb = (px - 0.04, y + h / 2), (x + w + 0.04, y + h / 2)
                else:
                    a, bb = (px + w / 2, py - 0.04), (x + w / 2, y + h + 0.04)
                ax.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch(a, bb, arrowstyle="-|>", mutation_scale=30, color=MUTED, lw=3,
                                             connectionstyle="arc3,rad=0"))
            prev = (x, y)
    fig.savefig(OUT / "poster_pipeline_diagram.png", dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)


def fig_pr():

    ax.set_xlabel("Recall"); ax.set_ylabel("Precision")

def show_rrim_613(ax, b):

def fig_transfer():
    """Tile 613590: the 9t-trained pit and pad models run on a tile they never saw. Predictions with hand outlines where drawn."""
    inf = D613 / "inference_05"
    full = (613500, 4590000, 618000, 4594500)
    pit_h, pad_h = ann("pit_inside"), ann("plat")
    W, H = 660, 420
    best = None
    for x0 in range(613500, 618000 - W, 60):
        for y0 in range(4590000, 4594500 - H, 60):
            b = (x0, y0, x0 + W, y0 + H)
            n = len(pit_h.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]]) + len(pad_h.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]])
            if best is None or n > best[0]:
                best = (n, b)
    b = best[1]
    pit_m = read(inf / "pit_argmax_613590_05.tif", b) == 1
    pad_m = read(inf / "pad_argmax_613590_05.tif", b) == 1
    hits, miss, fa = classify(pit_m, pit_h.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]], b)
    fa = [f for f in fa if b[0] + 40 < f[0] < b[2] - 40 and b[1] + 40 < f[1] < b[3] - 40]
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(13.47, 5.0), dpi=DPI)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0.105, 0.16, 0.50, 0.82])
    show_rrim_613(ax, b)
    overlay(ax, pad_m, b, MPAD, 0.30)
    overlay(ax, pit_m, b, MPIT, 0.80)
    for g, col, ls in ((pad_h, MPAD, "--"), (pit_h, MPIT, "-")):
        gg = g.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]]
        if len(gg):
            gg.boundary.plot(ax=ax, color=col, linewidth=1.6, linestyle=ls)
    ax.set_xlim(b[0], b[2]); ax.set_ylim(b[1], b[3])
    ax.set_xticks(np.arange(np.ceil(b[0] / 200) * 200, b[2] + 1, 200))
    ax.set_yticks(np.arange(np.ceil(b[1] / 200) * 200, b[3] + 1, 200))
    ax.ticklabel_format(useOffset=False, style="plain")
    ax.tick_params(labelsize=17, length=6, width=1.2)
    ax.set_xlabel("Easting (m), EPSG:6346", fontsize=19)
    ax.set_ylabel("Northing (m)", fontsize=19)
    ax.text(0.015, 0.975, "(a)", transform=ax.transAxes, va="top", fontsize=24, color="black", path_effects=HALO)
    sci_scalebar(ax, b[2] - 190, b[1] + 60, 100, fs=16, nseg=2)
    picks = []
    if hits: picks.append(("b", max(hits, key=lambda g: g.area).centroid))
    if fa:
        f = max(fa, key=lambda t_: t_[2]); picks.append(("c", type("P", (), {"x": f[0], "y": f[1]})))
    half = 40
    for i, (lab, c) in enumerate(picks):
        ib = (c.x - half, c.y - half, c.x + half, c.y + half)
        ax.add_patch(Rectangle((ib[0], ib[1]), 2 * half, 2 * half, fill=False, ec="black", lw=2))
        ax.text(ib[2] + 6, ib[3], lab, fontsize=22, color="black", va="top", path_effects=HALO)
        side = 0.34
        ia = fig.add_axes([0.635 + i * 0.185, 0.50, 0.175, 0.175 * 13.47 / 5.0])
        show_rrim_613(ia, ib)
        pm = read(inf / "pit_argmax_613590_05.tif", ib) == 1
        pd_ = read(inf / "pad_argmax_613590_05.tif", ib) == 1
        overlay(ia, pd_, ib, MPAD, 0.30); overlay(ia, pm, ib, MPIT, 0.80)
        for gdf, col, ls in ((pad_h, MPAD, "--"), (pit_h, MPIT, "-")):
            gg = gdf.cx[ib[0]:ib[2], ib[1]:ib[3]]
            if len(gg):
                gg.boundary.plot(ax=ia, color=col, linewidth=2, linestyle=ls)
        ia.set_xlim(ib[0], ib[2]); ia.set_ylim(ib[1], ib[3]); ia.set_xticks([]); ia.set_yticks([])
        for s in ia.spines.values():
            s.set_linewidth(1.2); s.set_color("black")
        ia.text(0.05, 0.95, f"({lab})", transform=ia.transAxes, va="top", fontsize=20, color="black", path_effects=HALO)
        sci_scalebar(ia, ib[0] + 6, ib[1] + 14, 20, fs=11, nseg=2)
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch
    handles = [Patch(fc=MPIT, ec="none", alpha=0.8, label="Pit, model output"),
               Line2D([0], [0], color=MPIT, lw=2.5, label="Pit, hand-drawn"),
               Patch(fc=MPAD, ec="none", alpha=0.3, label="Pad, model output"),
               Line2D([0], [0], color=MPAD, lw=2.5, ls="--", label="Pad, hand-drawn")]
    lg = fig.legend(handles=handles, loc="upper left", bbox_to_anchor=(0.635, 0.44), fontsize=17, frameon=True,
                    fancybox=False, edgecolor="black", framealpha=1.0, borderpad=0.7, labelspacing=0.7)
    lg.get_frame().set_linewidth(1.2)
    fig.savefig(OUT / "poster_transfer_613590.png", dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)
    return b, len(hits), len(miss), len(fa)


def fig_probmaps():
    """Tile 613590, 400 m window: (a) relief image with hand outlines, (b) pit and (c) pad probability, grey scale 0 to 1."""
    inf = D613 / "inference_05"
    pit_h, pad_h = ann("pit_inside"), ann("plat")
    S = 400
    best = None
    for x0 in range(613500, 618000 - S, 50):
        for y0 in range(4590000, 4594500 - S, 50):
            b = (x0, y0, x0 + S, y0 + S)
            n = len(pit_h.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]]) + len(pad_h.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]])
            if best is None or n > best[0]:
                best = (n, b)
    b = best[1]
    f613 = inf / "features_613590_05.tif"
    pp = prob_ensemble("pit", b, f613)
    dp = prob_ensemble("pad", b, f613)
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(13.47, 4.9), dpi=DPI)
    fig.subplots_adjust(left=0.105, right=0.985, top=0.97, bottom=0.25, wspace=0.07)
    ext = (b[0], b[2], b[1], b[3])
    show_rrim_613(axs[0], b)
    for g, col, ls in ((pad_h, MPAD, "--"), (pit_h, MPIT, "-")):
        gg = g.cx[b[0]:b[2], b[1]:b[3]]
        if len(gg):
            gg.boundary.plot(ax=axs[0], color=col, linewidth=2.0, linestyle=ls)
    im = axs[1].imshow(pp, cmap="gray", vmin=0, vmax=1, extent=ext, interpolation="nearest")
    axs[2].imshow(dp, cmap="gray", vmin=0, vmax=1, extent=ext, interpolation="nearest")
    for i, (ax, lab) in enumerate(zip(axs, "abc")):
        ax.set_xlim(b[0], b[2]); ax.set_ylim(b[1], b[3])
        ax.set_xticks([b[0] + 100, b[0] + 300]); ax.set_yticks([])
        ax.ticklabel_format(useOffset=False, style="plain")
        ax.tick_params(labelsize=15, length=5, width=1.2)
        for s in ax.spines.values():
            s.set_linewidth(1.2); s.set_color("black")
        ax.text(0.03, 0.97, f"({lab})", transform=ax.transAxes, va="top", fontsize=22, color="black", path_effects=HALO)
    axs[0].set_yticks([b[1] + 100, b[1] + 300]); axs[0].set_ylabel("Northing (m)", fontsize=16)
    axs[0].tick_params(axis="y", labelsize=15)
    axs[1].set_xlabel("Easting (m), EPSG:6346", fontsize=17)
    sci_scalebar(axs[0], b[0] + 20, b[1] + 55, 100, fs=13, nseg=2)
    cax = fig.add_axes([0.76, 0.135, 0.22, 0.03])
    cb = fig.colorbar(im, cax=cax, orientation="horizontal", ticks=[0, 0.5, 1])
    cb.ax.tick_params(labelsize=14, length=4); cb.set_label("Probability", fontsize=15, labelpad=2)
    fig.savefig(OUT / "poster_prob_maps_613590.png", dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)
    return b, float(pp.max()), float(dp.max())


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2026-11-27: four-panel hero (aerial, RRIM, pad probability, pit probability), Hann-blended
import sys as _sys
_sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "notebooks/wellsight_v2"))
import torch  # noqa: E402
from _dl import DEVICE, UNet, predict_array  # noqa: E402
import requests  # noqa: E402
from pyproj import Transformer  # noqa: E402

FEAT9 = D05 / "features_pit_9t_05.tif"
TASKS_CV = {"pit": ("pit/unet_cv5", 3, 256, 128), "pad": ("pad/unet_cv5", 2, 384, 192)}
_MODEL_CACHE = {}
PADM = 96.0


def _load_fold_model(task, k):
    key = (task, k)
    if key not in _MODEL_CACHE:
        sub, ncls, _, _ = TASKS_CV[task]
        ck = torch.load(MOD / sub / f"fold{k}" / "best.pt", map_location=DEVICE, weights_only=False)
        m = UNet(in_ch=len(ck["channels"]), n_classes=ncls, base=32).to(DEVICE)
        m.load_state_dict(ck["state_dict"])
        m.eval()
        _MODEL_CACHE[key] = (m, np.asarray(ck["mu"], np.float32), np.asarray(ck["sd"], np.float32))
    return _MODEL_CACHE[key]


def read_feats(path, bounds):
    """Seven-channel stack over bounds plus PADM metres of context each side, 0.5 m."""
    pb = (bounds[0] - PADM, bounds[1] - PADM, bounds[2] + PADM, bounds[3] + PADM)
    with rasterio.open(path) as r:
        w = from_bounds(*pb, r.transform)
        a = r.read(window=w, boundless=True, fill_value=np.nan).astype(np.float32)
    return a


def crop_ctx(arr):
    i = int(round(PADM / 0.5))
    return arr[..., i:-i, i:-i]


def prob_heldout(task, bounds):
    """Out-of-fold class-1 probability over bounds on 9t: each pixel from the model that never saw its block. Hann blended."""
    feats = read_feats(FEAT9, bounds)
    tr, shape = grid(bounds)
    bf = block_fold(task)
    fold = rasterize(((g, int(f)) for g, f in zip(bf.geometry, bf.fold)), shape, transform=tr, fill=-1)
    _, ncls, patch, ov = TASKS_CV[task]
    out = np.full(shape, np.nan, np.float32)
    for k in sorted(set(np.unique(fold)) - {-1}):
        m, mu, sd = _load_fold_model(task, k)
        p = crop_ctx(predict_array(m, feats, mu, sd, patch=patch, overlap=ov, n_classes=ncls, hann=True)[1])
        out[fold == k] = p[fold == k]
    return out


def prob_ensemble(task, bounds, feat_path):
    """Mean of the five fold models, Hann blended. For a tile none of them saw."""
    feats = read_feats(feat_path, bounds)
    _, ncls, patch, ov = TASKS_CV[task]
    acc = 0
    for k in range(5):
        m, mu, sd = _load_fold_model(task, k)
        acc = acc + predict_array(m, feats, mu, sd, patch=patch, overlap=ov, n_classes=ncls, hann=True)[1]
    return crop_ctx(acc / 5.0)


def naip(bounds, year="20220619"):
    """NAIP leaf-on aerial over bounds (EPSG:6346) from Planetary Computer, windowed read. Returns RGB uint8."""
    stac = "https://planetarycomputer.microsoft.com/api/stac/v1"
    cx, cy = (bounds[0] + bounds[2]) / 2, (bounds[1] + bounds[3]) / 2
    ll = gpd.GeoSeries(gpd.points_from_xy([cx], [cy]), crs=6346).to_crs(4326).iloc[0]
    items = requests.post(f"{stac}/search", json={"collections": ["naip"], "intersects": {"type": "Point", "coordinates": [ll.x, ll.y]}, "limit": 20}, timeout=60).json()["features"]
    by = {f["properties"]["datetime"][:10].replace("-", ""): f for f in items}
    f = by[year]
    href = requests.get("https://planetarycomputer.microsoft.com/api/sas/v1/sign?href=" + f["assets"]["image"]["href"], timeout=60).json()["href"]
    with rasterio.open(href) as s:
        t = Transformer.from_crs(6346, s.crs, always_xy=True)
        x0, y0 = t.transform(bounds[0], bounds[1])
        x1, y1 = t.transform(bounds[2], bounds[3])
        win = from_bounds(min(x0, x1), min(y0, y1), max(x0, x1), max(y0, y1), transform=s.transform)
        a = s.read([1, 2, 3], window=win, boundless=True, fill_value=0)
    return np.moveaxis(a, 0, -1)


def pick_window4(S=400):
    """S x S window on a 50 m step with held-out coverage for both tasks everywhere, most annotated pits and pads."""
    from shapely.geometry import box
    pbk, dbk = block_fold("pit"), block_fold("pad")
    pits, pads = ann("pit_inside"), ann("plat")
    best = []
    for x0 in range(619500, 624000 - S + 1, 50):
        for y0 in range(4593000, 4597500 - S + 1, 50):
            bb = box(x0, y0, x0 + S, y0 + S)
            if pbk.geometry.intersection(bb).area.sum() < 0.999 * bb.area or dbk.geometry.intersection(bb).area.sum() < 0.999 * bb.area:
                continue
            npit = len(pits.cx[x0:x0 + S, y0:y0 + S])
            npad = len(pads.cx[x0:x0 + S, y0:y0 + S])
            if npit >= 6 and npad >= 3:
                best.append((npit + npad, (x0, y0, x0 + S, y0 + S)))
    best.sort(reverse=True)
    return [b for _, b in best]


def show_rrim_9(ax, b):
    with rasterio.open(D05 / "rrim_openness_9t_05.tif") as r:
        w = from_bounds(*b, r.transform)
        a = np.moveaxis(r.read(window=w, boundless=True, fill_value=0), 0, -1)
    ax.imshow(a, extent=(b[0], b[2], b[1], b[3]), interpolation="bilinear")


def fig_hero4(b=None):
    pit_h, pad_h = ann("pit_inside"), ann("plat")
    b = b or pick_window4()[0]
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = b
    pit_p = prob_heldout("pit", b)
    pad_p = prob_heldout("pad", b)
    air = naip(b)
    W, H = 27.6, 8.0
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(W, H), dpi=DPI)
    side, gap, left, bottom = 6.05, 0.33, 1.95, 1.75
    axs = [fig.add_axes([(left + i * (side + gap)) / W, bottom / H, side / W, side / H]) for i in range(4)]
    ext = (x0, x1, y0, y1)
    axs[0].imshow(air, extent=ext, interpolation="bilinear")
    show_rrim_9(axs[1], b)
    im = axs[2].imshow(pad_p, cmap="gray", vmin=0, vmax=1, extent=ext, interpolation="nearest")
    axs[3].imshow(pit_p, cmap="gray", vmin=0, vmax=1, extent=ext, interpolation="nearest")
    for g, ax, col, ls in ((pad_h, axs[2], MPAD, "--"), (pit_h, axs[3], MPIT, "-")):
        gg = g.cx[x0:x1, y0:y1]
        if len(gg):
            gg.boundary.plot(ax=ax, color=col, linewidth=2.0, linestyle=ls)
    for i, ax in enumerate(axs):
        ax.set_xlim(x0, x1)
        ax.set_ylim(y0, y1)
        ax.set_xticks([x0 + 100, x0 + 300])
        ax.set_yticks([y0 + 100, y0 + 300] if i == 0 else [])
        ax.ticklabel_format(useOffset=False, style="plain")
        ax.tick_params(labelsize=22, length=7, width=1.5)
        for s in ax.spines.values():
            s.set_linewidth(1.5)
            s.set_color("black")
        ax.text(0.03, 0.97, "(" + "abcd"[i] + ")", transform=ax.transAxes, va="top", fontsize=34, color="black", path_effects=HALO)
    fig.text(0.40 / W, (bottom + side / 2) / H, "Northing (m)", rotation=90, va="center", ha="center", fontsize=24)
    fig.text((left + side + gap / 2) / W, 0.80 / H, "Easting (m), EPSG:6346", ha="center", fontsize=24)
    sci_scalebar(axs[1], x0 + 20, y0 + 52, 100, fs=20, nseg=2)
    axs[1].annotate("", xy=(x1 - 25, y1 - 22), xytext=(x1 - 25, y1 - 75), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle="-|>", color="white", lw=3, mutation_scale=30))
    axs[1].text(x1 - 25, y1 - 82, "N", ha="center", va="top", fontsize=28, color="white", path_effects=[pe.withStroke(linewidth=5, foreground="black")])
    cax = fig.add_axes([(left + 2 * (side + gap) + 0.6) / W, 0.95 / H, (2 * side + gap - 1.2) / W, 0.22 / H])
    cb = fig.colorbar(im, cax=cax, orientation="horizontal", ticks=[0, 0.5, 1])
    cb.ax.tick_params(labelsize=22, length=6)
    cb.set_label("Probability", fontsize=24, labelpad=4)
    fig.savefig(OUT / "poster_hero4_9t.png", dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)
    return b


def fig_errors():
    """Found pit, missed pit, model pit with no hand label: three 50 m windows from one held-out window, F2 cutoff."""
    pits_rim, pads = ann("pit_outside"), ann("plat")
    pit_h = ann("pit_inside")
    for bw in pick_window()[:25]:
        b = (bw[0], bw[1] + 31, bw[2], bw[3] - 32)
        pit_m, pad_m, cover = oof(b)
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = b
        m = 30
        hits, miss, fa = classify(pit_m, pits_rim.cx[x0 + m:x1 - m, y0 + m:y1 - m], b)
        fa = [f for f in fa if x0 + m < f[0] < x1 - m and y0 + m < f[1] < y1 - m]
        if hits and miss and fa:
            break
    f = max(fa, key=lambda t_: t_[2])
    picks = [max(hits, key=lambda g: g.area).centroid, miss[0].centroid, type("P", (), {"x": f[0], "y": f[1]})]
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(13.47, 4.7), dpi=DPI)
    fig.subplots_adjust(left=0.01, right=0.99, top=0.99, bottom=0.01, wspace=0.03)
    half = 25
    for i, (ax, c) in enumerate(zip(axs, picks)):
        ib = (c.x - half, c.y - half, c.x + half, c.y + half)
        show_hs(ax, ib)
        pm, _, _ = oof(ib)
        overlay(ax, pm, ib, MPIT, 0.75)
        for gdf, col, ls in ((pads, MPAD, "--"), (pit_h, MPIT, "-")):
            gg = gdf.cx[ib[0]:ib[2], ib[1]:ib[3]]
            if len(gg):
                gg.boundary.plot(ax=ax, color=col, linewidth=2.5, linestyle=ls)
        ax.set_xlim(ib[0], ib[2])
        ax.set_ylim(ib[1], ib[3])
        ax.set_xticks([])
        ax.set_yticks([])
        for s in ax.spines.values():
            s.set_linewidth(1.5)
            s.set_color("black")
        ax.text(0.04, 0.96, "(" + "abc"[i] + ")", transform=ax.transAxes, va="top", fontsize=30, color="black", path_effects=HALO)
        sci_scalebar(ax, ib[0] + 3, ib[1] + 7, 10, fs=17, nseg=2)
    fig.savefig(OUT / "poster_errors_9t.png", dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)
    return b, len(hits), len(miss), len(fa)


def main():
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    for f in (fig_gallery, fig_folds, fig_optical):
        f(); print(f.__name__)
    print("hero4 window:", fig_hero4())
    print("errors window, hits, misses, unlabelled:", fig_errors())
    print("transfer 613590 window, max pit, max pad:", fig_probmaps())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
### agu_poster/_make_agu_poster.py
```python
"""AGU poster, 44 x 44 in, built on the user's UH template frame. The template is never edited.

Input   agu_poster/AGU_poster.pptx           user template (56 x 45 in, UH Cullen College frame)
        agu_poster/figures/*.png             built by agu_poster/_make_poster_figures.py
Output  agu_poster/agu_poster_wireframe_44in.pptx   grey blocks say what goes where
        agu_poster/agu_poster_filled_44in.pptx      the filled poster: figures, captions, text (--mockup)
        agu_poster/agu_poster_outline_44in.pptx     figures placed, every text slot a [stand-in] (--outline)

Layout follows the poster-design evidence in poster_agu_layout_draft.md section 10:
one main-finding sentence in large type above the hero (Silver et al. 2025 RCT, key-concept recall),
every section led by a figure, headings written as statements, methods and validation kept on the page
(Oronje et al. 2022; Bentsen and Ostergaard 2025), about 350 to 500 words.

Each section is a stack of blocks laid top to bottom: ("fig", key, height, wireframe note) or ("text", body).
"|" in body text starts a new paragraph.

Run
    python agu_poster/_make_agu_poster.py            # wireframe
    python agu_poster/_make_agu_poster.py --mockup   # with figures
    python agu_poster/_make_agu_poster.py --outline  # figures, stand-in text
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import io
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image
from pptx import Presentation
from pptx.dml.color import RGBColor
from pptx.enum.text import MSO_ANCHOR, PP_ALIGN
from pptx.util import Inches, Pt

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = HERE.parent
SRC = HERE / "AGU_poster.pptx"
FIGS = HERE / "figures"
OUT_WIRE = HERE / "agu_poster_wireframe_44in.pptx"
OUT_MOCK = HERE / "agu_poster_filled_44in.pptx"
OUT_OUTLINE = HERE / "agu_poster_outline_44in.pptx"
PAGE = 44.0
WHITE, GREY = RGBColor(0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF), RGBColor(0x2B, 0x2F, 0x36)
INK = RGBColor(0x2B, 0x2F, 0x36)         # charcoal body text
UH_RED = RGBColor(0xC0, 0x00, 0x00)
FIG_FILL = RGBColor(0xF2, 0xF2, 0xF2)
RULE = RGBColor(0xBF, 0xBF, 0xBF)

# grid (inches). No box outlines and no page border: white space separates sections.
X0, X1, GAP = 1.0, 43.0, 0.8
CW = (X1 - X0 - 2 * GAP) / 3
C1, C2, C3 = X0, X0 + CW + GAP, X0 + 2 * (CW + GAP)
HEAD = (0.8, 5.6)          # header band top, height (the one solid block of UH red)
BODY_TOP, BODY_BOT = 7.2, 40.0
FOOT_TOP, FOOT_BOT = 40.9, 43.2
HERO_H = 8.0               # hero figure height, figure is 27.6 x 9.4 in
LOWER = BODY_TOP + 3.6 + HERO_H + 0.9     # top of the col 2 / col 3 sections under the hero

HEAD_PT, BODY_PT, CAP_PT = 50, 28, 24
HEAD_H = 1.05              # one-line statement heading at 54 pt
LINE = BODY_PT * 1.2 / 72  # body line height, in
CHARS = 74                 # body characters per line across one column at 44 pt (Calibri, measured)

TITLE = "Detecting orphaned oil and gas wells under forest canopy with airborne lidar and U-Nets"
TAKEAWAY = "Airborne lidar finds 93% of well pits under forest canopy while flagging 0.13% of the ground"

FIG_FILES = {
    "intro": FIGS / "poster_pa_wells_locator.png",
    "signs": FIGS / "poster_signs_gallery_9t.png",
    "hero": FIGS / "poster_hero4_9t.png",
    "valid": FIGS / "poster_cv_folds_pit_9t.png",
    "transfer": FIGS / "poster_prob_maps_613590.png",
    "errors": FIGS / "poster_errors_9t.png",
}

# Numbered captions in reading order. Scale and data source are stated for every map.
CAPS = {
    "hero": ("Figure 1.", "One 400 m window of tile 9t, from models that never saw these blocks. (a) Leaf-on aerial photograph (NAIP, June 2022). "
             "(b) Red relief image map from bare-earth lidar. (c) Pad probability with hand-drawn pads (yellow dashed). "
             "(d) Pit probability with hand-drawn pits (blue). Probability runs from black 0 to white 1. Bar 100 m, north up."),
    "intro": ("Figure 2.", "Documented orphan and abandoned wells (yellow points) in western Pennsylvania, on a "
              "satellite base map. North is up. Source: Pennsylvania DEP records."),
    "signs": ("Figure 3.", "Hand-drawn labels on a red relief image map. From left to right: pit (blue), pad (yellow) and road (white). "
              "A pit is a bowl with a raised rim, a pad is the flat work area and a road is the track to it. Panels are 60, 180 and 180 m wide."),
    "valid": ("Figure 5.", "Spatial cross-validation on 375 m blocks. Blue blocks are held out, grey blocks train the model, white blocks are unused."),
    "transfer": ("Figure 4.", "Tile 613590, 1.5 km west of 9t and unseen in training (400 m window, mean of the five fold models). "
                 "(a) Relief map with hand outlines. (b) Pit probability. (c) Pad probability, black 0 to white 1."),
    "errors": ("Figure 6.", "Three 50 m windows from a held-out block of tile 9t. (a) Found pit. (b) Missed pit. "
               "(c) Model pit with no hand label, which counts against precision. Bars 10 m."),
}


SECTIONS = [
    ("Why orphan wells stay hidden", C1, BODY_TOP, [
        ("fig", "intro", 4.2, "Map of Pennsylvania documented orphan wells"),
        ("text", "Hazard. Thousands of undocumented wells in western Pennsylvania can leak methane and brine.|"
                 "Why current data fall short. Canopy hides about half of the pads from satellites (Ramachandran et al. 2024). "
                 "Legacy well coordinates can be tens to hundreds of metres off.|"
                 "Approach. Map the pit, pad and access road that every well leaves, directly from leaf-off USGS 3DEP lidar, "
                 "and ask how well a U-Net does it on ground it has not seen."),
    ]),
    ("Lidar data and terrain layers", C1, None, [
        ("fig", "signs", 4.0, "Pit, pad, road on RRIM"),
        ("text", "USGS 3DEP lidar, project PA_WesternPA_2019_D20, flown leaf-off Nov 2019 to Mar 2020, about 2.6 ground points per m2.|"
                 "Ground returns are triangulated (Delaunay) and gridded to a 0.5 m bare-earth model. Seven layers are derived: relief in 12.5 m "
                 "and 2.5 m windows, slope, topographic position, positive and negative openness, and roughness.|"
                 "We outlined 503 pits and 650 pads by hand in tile 9t (712 and 995 across all tiles), without the DEP well records."),
    ]),
    ("Transfer to an unseen tile", C1, None, [
        ("text", "Tile 613590 shares no ground with 9t. All five fold models run on it with the 9t cutoffs and 9t normalisation, nothing retuned. "
                 "Pit recall is in Results. Pads are not scored there because none are drawn."),
        ("fig", "transfer", 4.6, "Predictions on tile 613590"),
    ]),
    ("Methods", C2, LOWER, [
        ("spec", None, 2.3, None),
        ("text", "One plain four-level U-Net per sign, 7.8 million parameters, standardised inputs. Focal cross-entropy (gamma 2), "
                 "random flips and rotations, AdamW at 1e-3, cosine schedule, 40 epochs, batch 16. Drainage is an explicit negative class "
                 "for roads. Probabilities above the cutoff are vectorised.|"
                 "A 26 million parameter U-Net++ with a pretrained encoder scored no better on pits (pixel IoU 0.561 against 0.559), "
                 "so data and labels limit accuracy, not architecture."),
    ]),
    ("Validation and transfer", C2, None, [
        ("fig", "valid", 2.0, "Five test sets of 375 m blocks"),
        ("text", "Five-fold cross-validation on 375 m blocks, split by block and never by well, so a jittered patch cannot reach a held-out block.|"
                 "An inner validation set picks the epoch and the F2 cutoff, which weights recall four times as heavily as precision. "
                 "The held-out fold never tunes either."),
    ]),
    ("Conclusions", C2, None, [
        ("text", "Terrain layers from leaf-off lidar let a small U-Net find more than 90% of labelled pits and pads in blocks it never saw, "
                 "and 91% of pits on a separate tile.|"
                 "Limits. One survey, one annotator, precision below 0.65, no field visits.|"
                 "Next. Field-check candidates, combine pit, pad and road co-occurrence to cut false alarms, and test on McKean lidar."),
    ]),
    ("Results", C3, LOWER, [
        ("table", None, 3.6, None),
        ("fig", "errors", 4.5, "Found, missed, unlabelled"),
        ("text", "Pits and pads are found at 93% and 91% (467 of 503, 593 of 650). Threshold-free average precision at IoU 0.3 is 0.82 for pits "
                 "(95% bootstrap interval 0.78 to 0.86) and 0.75 for pads (0.70 to 0.79).|"
                 "Pad recall falls to 77% at IoU 0.5. Where a pad ends and a wide road begins is an arbitrary line, so pad edges differ from ours "
                 "even when the pad is found. Precision is a lower bound, since some unmatched detections may be real features we did not draw.|"
                 "On tile 613590 the pit models recover 91% of the 153 pits drawn there (standard deviation 4.5 points over the five models), "
                 "against 93% on 9t. About 30% of that tile is undrawn, so precision is not scored.|"
                 "Pits are the best target: highest average precision, sharpest outlines and the smallest search area."),
    ]),
]
# Results table: ann712 CV5, F2 threshold per fold, IoU 0.3 (docs/iterations/LEADERBOARD.md, 2026-09-17).
# Roads: recall_clean at thr 0.20 on 735 held-out segments; road precision is not object-scored, so it is left blank.
TABLE = [("", "Recall", "Precision", "IoU 0.5 recall", "Ground flagged"),
         ("Pits", "93%", "63%", "82%", "0.13%"),
         ("Pads", "91%", "59%", "77%", "10%"),
         ("Roads\u2020", "98%", "\u2014", "\u2014", "5.0%")]
TABLE_NOTE = ("Table 1. Held-out results, tile 9t, IoU 0.3 and F2 cutoff unless stated. A detection matches a hand outline when intersection over union (IoU) "
              "is at least 0.3. Ground flagged is the share of scored ground above the cutoff. \u2020Roads: 40 m chunks whose whole road was held out, "
              "single-split model, probability 0.20, no precision. On tile 613590, roads drawn there and unseen in training reach completeness 0.76 and correctness 0.83 at probability 0.50.")
SPEC = [("Sign", "Grid, patch", "Classes", "Focal weights"),
        ("Pits", "0.5 m, 128 m", "background, floor, rim", "0.05, 0.475, 0.475"),
        ("Pads", "0.5 m, 192 m", "background, pad", "0.15, 0.85"),
        ("Roads", "1 m, 256 m", "background, road, drainage", "0.10, 0.72, 0.25")]
# --outline: stand-in text per section, (heading, [one entry per text block]). Space is kept at the real
# text's height so the layout matches the mockup.
OUTLINE = [
    ("[Heading: why the problem matters]",
     ["[Background: what orphan wells are and why they matter]|[Prior work here: satellite and lidar "
      "studies, then the question]"]),
    ("[Heading: why lidar]", ["[Data: lidar source, area, flight dates, terrain layers built]"]),
    ("[Heading: what a well leaves behind]", ["[Signs: describe pit, pad and road. Number of hand labels]"]),
    ("[Heading: the model]", ["[Method: model type, outputs, cutoff, how roads differ]"]),
    ("[Heading: how it was tested]", ["[Validation: blocks, test sets, match rule, road test set]"]),
    ("[Heading: main result]", ["[Results: what the numbers say, where it is weak and why]"]),
    ("[Heading: implications]", ["[Implications here]|[Limits here]|[Next steps here]"]),
]
OUTLINE_TABLE = [TABLE[0]] + [(r[0], "[xx%]", "[xx%]", "[xx%]", "[xx%]") for r in TABLE[1:]]

HERO_CAPTION = CAPS["hero"][1]


def named(slide, name):
    return [s for s in slide.shapes if s.name == name]


def place(shape, x, y, w, h):
    shape.left, shape.top, shape.width, shape.height = Inches(x), Inches(y), Inches(w), Inches(h)


def text_height(text, size=BODY_PT, chars=CHARS):
    """Rough height of wrapped body text in a column, in inches."""
    k = chars * BODY_PT / size
    lines = sum(max(1, -(-len(p) // int(k))) for p in text.split("|"))
    paras = len(text.split("|"))
    return lines * size * 1.2 / 72 + (paras - 1) * size * 0.4 / 72


def text_box(slide, x, y, w, h, text, size, color, bold=False, italic=False, align=PP_ALIGN.LEFT,
             anchor=MSO_ANCHOR.TOP, lead=None):
    tb = slide.shapes.add_textbox(Inches(x), Inches(y), Inches(w), Inches(h))
    tf = tb.text_frame
    tf.word_wrap = True
    tf.vertical_anchor = anchor
    tf.margin_left = tf.margin_right = tf.margin_top = tf.margin_bottom = 0
    for i, line in enumerate(text.split("|")):
        p = tf.paragraphs[0] if i == 0 else tf.add_paragraph()
        p.alignment = align
        p.space_after = Pt(size * 0.4)
        if lead and i == 0:
            r0 = p.add_run()
            r0.text = lead + " "
            r0.font.size, r0.font.bold, r0.font.italic, r0.font.name = Pt(size), False, False, "Georgia"
            r0.font.color.rgb = color
        r = p.add_run()
        r.text = line
        r.font.size, r.font.bold, r.font.italic, r.font.name = Pt(size), False, italic, "Georgia"
        r.font.color.rgb = color
    return tb


def load_image(path: Path):
    """Downscale to at most 4000 px on the long side and return JPEG or PNG bytes plus the pixel size."""
    im = Image.open(path).convert("RGB")
    im.thumbnail((4000, 4000))
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    im.save(buf, "PNG", optimize=True)
    buf.seek(0)
    return buf, im.size


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--mockup", action="store_true", help="place the built figures")
    ap.add_argument("--outline", action="store_true", help="figures placed, every text slot a stand-in")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    outline = args.outline
    mock = args.mockup or outline

    prs = Presentation(str(SRC))
    prs.slide_width = prs.slide_height = Inches(PAGE)
    s = prs.slides[0]
    band, logo = named(s, "Rectangle 7")[0], named(s, "Picture 59")[0]
    keep = {band._element, logo._element}
    for sh in list(s.shapes):
        if sh._element not in keep:
            sh._element.getparent().remove(sh._element)

    place(band, 0.0, 0.0, PAGE, HEAD[0] + HEAD[1])      # full-bleed band, no outer border
    lw, lh = logo.width / 914400, logo.height / 914400
    place(logo, X0, (HEAD[0] + HEAD[1] - lh) / 2, lw, lh)
    tx = X0 + lw + 0.8
    text_box(s, tx, HEAD[0] + 0.3, X1 - tx, 3.0, "[Poster title]" if outline else TITLE, 88, WHITE, bold=True)
    text_box(s, tx, HEAD[0] + 3.5, X1 - tx, 1.0, "[Authors]" if outline else "Colton Goodrich", 50, WHITE)
    text_box(s, tx, HEAD[0] + 4.5, X1 - tx, 0.8,
             "[Affiliation]" if outline else "Cullen College of Engineering, University of Houston", 38, WHITE)

    def rect(x, y, w, h, color):
        r = s.shapes.add_shape(1, Inches(x), Inches(y), Inches(w), Inches(h))
        r.fill.solid(); r.fill.fore_color.rgb = color
        r.line.fill.background()
        r.shadow.inherit = False
        return r

    def figure(key, x, y, w, h, note, size=36):
        """Place the figure, then its numbered caption. Returns the caption height in inches."""
        figure_img(key, x, y, w, h, note, size)
        if key in CAPS and not outline:
            lead, cap = CAPS[key]
            ch = text_height(lead + " " + cap, CAP_PT)
            text_box(s, x, y + h + 0.08, w, ch + 0.1, cap, CAP_PT, GREY, lead=lead)
            return ch + 0.15
        return 0.0

    def figure_img(key, x, y, w, h, note, size=36):
        """Grey block in the wireframe. In the mockup, the figure fitted inside the area, top-centred."""
        if mock and FIG_FILES.get(key, Path("-")).exists():
            buf, (pw, ph) = load_image(FIG_FILES[key])
            scale = min(w / pw, h / ph)
            fw, fh = pw * scale, ph * scale
            s.shapes.add_picture(buf, Inches(x + (w - fw) / 2), Inches(y), Inches(fw), Inches(fh))
            return
        rect(x, y, w, h, FIG_FILL)
        text_box(s, x + 0.5, y, w - 1.0, h, note, size, GREY, italic=True, align=PP_ALIGN.CENTER,
                 anchor=MSO_ANCHOR.MIDDLE)

    # thin grey rules break the page up without boxing anything in
    rw = 0.04
    rect(X0, FOOT_TOP - 0.45, X1 - X0, rw, RULE)
    rect(C2 - GAP / 2 - rw / 2, BODY_TOP, rw, BODY_BOT - BODY_TOP, RULE)
    rect(C3 - GAP / 2 - rw / 2, LOWER, rw, BODY_BOT - LOWER, RULE)

    # main finding, the one line a passer-by reads in 3 s; then the hero and its caption
    text_box(s, C2, BODY_TOP, X1 - C2, 3.2, "[Main finding in one sentence]" if outline else TAKEAWAY, 84, INK, bold=True, anchor=MSO_ANCHOR.MIDDLE)
    hero_y = BODY_TOP + 3.4
    figure_img("hero", C2, hero_y, X1 - C2, HERO_H,
           "Hero figure. Aerial, relief map, pad probability, pit probability, one 400 m window", 40)
    text_box(s, C2, hero_y + HERO_H + 0.05, X1 - C2, 0.6,
             "[Hero caption: what the map shows and where]" if outline else CAPS["hero"][1], CAP_PT, GREY,
             lead=None if outline else CAPS["hero"][0])

    words = len(TAKEAWAY.split()) + len(HERO_CAPTION.split())
    col_end = {C1: BODY_TOP - 0.7, C2: LOWER - 0.7, C3: LOWER - 0.7}
    for si, (head, x, y, blocks) in enumerate(SECTIONS):
        stand_texts = iter(OUTLINE[si][1])
        y = col_end[x] + 0.7 if y is None else y      # stack under the previous section in the column
        text_box(s, x, y, CW, HEAD_H, OUTLINE[si][0] if outline else head, HEAD_PT, INK, anchor=MSO_ANCHOR.BOTTOM)
        words += len(head.split())
        rule_y = y + HEAD_H + 0.12
        rect(x, rule_y, CW, 0.05, UH_RED)
        top = rule_y + 0.4
        for kind, key_or_text, h, note in [(b[0], b[1], b[2] if len(b) > 2 else None,
                                            b[3] if len(b) > 3 else None) for b in blocks]:
            if kind == "fig":
                ch = figure(key_or_text, x, top, CW, h, note)
                top += h + ch + 0.1
            elif kind == "spec":
                rh = h / len(SPEC)
                swd = [1.5, 3.0, 4.9, CW - 9.4]
                for r, row in enumerate(SPEC):
                    cx = x
                    for c, cell in enumerate(row):
                        text_box(s, cx, top + r * rh, swd[c], rh, cell, 26, INK if r else GREY, anchor=MSO_ANCHOR.MIDDLE)
                        cx += swd[c]
                    if r == 0:
                        rect(x, top + rh - 0.02, CW, 0.03, RULE)
                top += h + 0.3
            elif kind == "table":                       # recall and precision per sign, numbers large
                rh = (h - 0.6) / len(TABLE)
                nc = len(TABLE[0])
                cws = [2.3] + [(CW - 2.3) / (nc - 1)] * (nc - 1)
                for r, row in enumerate(OUTLINE_TABLE if outline else TABLE):
                    cx = x
                    for c, cell in enumerate(row):
                        big = r > 0 and c > 0
                        text_box(s, cx, top + r * rh, cws[c], rh, cell, 44 if big else 28,
                                 INK if (big or c == 0) else GREY, bold=big or c == 0,
                                 align=PP_ALIGN.LEFT if c == 0 else PP_ALIGN.CENTER, anchor=MSO_ANCHOR.MIDDLE)
                        cx += cws[c]
                    if r == 0:
                        rect(x, top + rh - 0.02, CW, 0.03, RULE)
                text_box(s, x, top + len(TABLE) * rh + 0.05, CW, 2.4,
                         "[Table note: define each column]" if outline else TABLE_NOTE[9:], 24, GREY,
                         lead=None if outline else "Table 1.")
                top += h + 1.7
            else:
                th = text_height(key_or_text)
                text_box(s, x, top, CW, th + 0.2, next(stand_texts) if outline else key_or_text, BODY_PT,
                         GREY if outline else INK, italic=outline)
                words += len(key_or_text.replace("|", " ").split())
                top += th + 0.3
        col_end[x] = top - 0.3
        if top - 0.3 > BODY_BOT + 0.05:
            print(f"WARNING: section '{head}' runs to y {top:.1f} in, past the body bottom {BODY_BOT}")

    # footer
    for head, x, w, note in (("References", X0, 19.0, "Ramachandran et al. 2024, Nat. Commun. 15, 7036. Saint-Vincent et al. 2021, J. Environ. Manage. 280, 111856. Sams et al., NETL, LiDAR mapping of 1860s oil fields, Pioneer Run (OSTI 1569995). USGS 3D Elevation Program"),
                             ("Acknowledgements", X0 + 19.8, 14.0, "Lidar from the USGS 3D Elevation Program, project PA_WesternPA_2019_D20. Well records from the Pennsylvania Department of Environmental Protection. Funding: [to add].")):
        text_box(s, x, FOOT_TOP - 0.1, w, 0.8, head, 40, INK)
        rect(x, FOOT_TOP + 0.75, w, 0.05, UH_RED)
        ref = head == "References"
        if outline:
            note = "[References here]" if ref else "[Funding and data credits here]"
        text_box(s, x, FOOT_TOP + 1.0, w, FOOT_BOT - FOOT_TOP - 0.95, note, 24 if ref else 28,
                 INK if ref else GREY, italic=not ref)
    text_box(s, X1 - 8.6, FOOT_TOP + 0.4, 5.9, 1.6, "Full methods, data and code", 32, INK,
             align=PP_ALIGN.RIGHT, anchor=MSO_ANCHOR.MIDDLE)
    rect(X1 - 2.3, FOOT_TOP, 2.3, 2.3, FIG_FILL)
    text_box(s, X1 - 2.3, FOOT_TOP, 2.3, 2.3, "QR", 28, GREY, italic=True, align=PP_ALIGN.CENTER,
             anchor=MSO_ANCHOR.MIDDLE)

    out = OUT_OUTLINE if outline else OUT_MOCK if mock else OUT_WIRE
    prs.save(str(out))
    print(out, f"body words {words}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
### notebooks/wellsight_v2/s7_analysis/_lidar_methodology_appendix.py
```python
"""Build the full-metadata + full-code appendix for the lidar pit / pad / road pipeline. Set 2026-11-26.

Why: docs/lidar_methodology_brief.md is the terse methodology. This dumps everything behind it, machine
generated so nothing is retyped: source tile index, raster profiles, checkpoint contents, annotation
schemas and counts, fold tables, result files verbatim, run order and the full source of every
script in the chain. The raw LAZ tiles are not on disk (offloaded), so source-lidar metadata comes from
data/_source/lidar/laz_tile_index.csv, not from LAS headers.
Out: docs/lidar_methodology_appendix.md
Run: python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s7_analysis/_lidar_methodology_appendix.py
"""
from pathlib import Path

import geopandas as gpd
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import rasterio

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
OUT = ROOT / "docs" / "lidar_methodology_appendix.md"
B9T = (619500, 4593000, 624000, 4597500)
B613 = (613500, 4590000, 618000, 4594500)
ANN = ROOT / "qgis/annotations/annotations_proj.gpkg"

SCRIPTS = [
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/_common.py", "notebooks/wellsight_v2/_dl.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s1_build/_build_derivatives.py", "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s1_build/_make_rrim.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s1_build/_build_extra_channels.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s2_labels/_build_label_grids.py", "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s2_labels/_build_pit_dataset_v2.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s2_labels/_build_pad_road_dataset.py", "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s2_labels/_build_unified_split.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s2_labels/_prep_road_1m.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s3_train/_pit_unet_cv5.py", "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s3_train/_pad_unet_cv5.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s3_train/_road_unet_1m_recall.py", "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s3_train/_drainage_unet_1m.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s4_infer/_infer_pit_pad_613590_05.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_threshold_common.py", "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_match_rules_pit_pad_9t.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_cv5_pr_sweep_records_pit_pad_9t.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_cv5_pr_calibration_pit_pad_9t.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_pad_cv5_tau_scale.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_transfer_pit_cv5_to_613590_05.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_is_613590_fully_annotated.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_score_road_pred_vs_roads_shp_613590.py",
    "agu_poster/_make_poster_figures.py", "agu_poster/_make_agu_poster.py",
    "notebooks/wellsight_v2/s7_analysis/_lidar_methodology_appendix.py",
]
RASTERS = [
    "data/9t/derived/05/dem_9t_05.tif", "data/9t/derived/05/chm_9t_05.tif", "data/9t/derived/05/hillshade_9t_05.tif",
    "data/9t/derived/05/rrim_openness_9t_05.tif", "data/9t/derived/05/features_pit_9t_05.tif",
    "data/9t/derived/05/labels_pit_9t_05.tif", "data/9t/models/pit/unet_cv5/pit_prob_floor_outoffold_9t_05.tif",
    "data/9t/models/pad/unet_cv5/fold0/pad_prob_cvfold0_9t_05.tif",
    "data/613590/derived/05/dem_613590_05.tif", "data/613590/derived/05/rrim_openness_613590_05.tif",
    "data/613590/derived/inference_05/features_613590_05.tif", "data/613590/derived/inference_05/pit_prob_floor_613590_05.tif",
    "data/613590/derived/inference_05/pad_prob_613590_05.tif", "data/613590/derived/inference_05/pit_argmax_613590_05.tif",
    "data/9t/derived/1m/dem_9t_1m.tif",
]
CKPTS = ([f"data/9t/models/pit/unet_cv5/fold{k}/best.pt" for k in range(5)]
         + [f"data/9t/models/pad/unet_cv5/fold{k}/best.pt" for k in range(5)]
         + ["data/9t/models/pit/unet_v2/best.pt", "data/9t/models/pad/unet/best.pt",
            "data/9t/models/road/unet_1m_recall/best.pt"])
VERBATIM = [
    "data/9t/derived/05/feature_stats.json",
    "data/9t/models/pit/unet_cv5/pit_cv5_per_fold_9t.csv", "data/9t/models/pad/unet_cv5/pad_cv5_per_fold_9t.csv",
    "data/9t/models/pit/unet_cv5/pit_cv5_recovery_curve_9t.csv",
    "data/613590/results/annotation_coverage_613590_vs_9t.json",
    "data/_comparisons/pit_heldout_and_transfer_2026-09-20/613590_transfer_from_9t_cv5_05/pit_transfer_recall_summary_613590_05.json",
    "data/9t/models/pit/unet_v2/test_metrics.json", "data/9t/models/pad/unet/test_metrics.json",
]
RUN = [
    "# 1 derivatives (PDAL merge -> DEM -> channels), 0.5 m, per tile",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s1_build/_build_derivatives.py --suffix 9t_05 --res 0.5 --dem-method delaunay --bbox 619500,4593000,624000,4597500 --tiles <glob>",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s1_build/_build_derivatives.py --suffix 613590_05 --res 0.5 --crs EPSG:6346 --dem-method delaunay --bbox 613500,4590000,618000,4594500 --tiles <glob>",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s1_build/_make_rrim.py            # RRIM from openness + slope",
    "# 2 labels and split (hand annotation in QGIS -> rasters, blocks, manifests). --force re-assigns folds: do not",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s2_labels/_build_pit_dataset_v2.py",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s2_labels/_build_pad_road_dataset.py",
    "# 3 train 5-fold block CV (40 epochs, F1 and F2 thresholds on inner validation)",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s3_train/_pit_unet_cv5.py --folds 5 --epochs 40",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s3_train/_pad_unet_cv5.py --folds 5 --epochs 40",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s3_train/_road_unet_1m_recall.py --epochs 40",
    "# 4 scoring",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_pad_cv5_tau_scale.py",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_cv5_pr_sweep_records_pit_pad_9t.py",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_cv5_pr_calibration_pit_pad_9t.py",
    "# 5 transfer to 613590",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s4_infer/_infer_pit_pad_613590_05.py",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s5_eval/_transfer_pit_cv5_to_613590_05.py",
    "# 6 poster",
    "python agu_poster/_make_poster_figures.py",
    "python agu_poster/_make_agu_poster.py --mockup",
    "python notebooks/wellsight_v2/s7_analysis/_lidar_methodology_appendix.py",
]


def prof(p):
    with rasterio.open(p) as s:
        return (f"crs {s.crs.to_string() if s.crs else None}, res {s.res}, size {s.width}x{s.height}, bands {s.count}, "
                f"dtype {s.dtypes[0]}, nodata {s.nodata}, bounds {tuple(round(b, 2) for b in s.bounds)}, "
                f"descriptions {s.descriptions}, tags {s.tags()}")


def main():
    L = []
    w = L.append
    w("# Lidar pit / pad / road pipeline appendix: full metadata and code\n")
    w("Machine generated by `notebooks/wellsight_v2/s7_analysis/_lidar_methodology_appendix.py`. Read with "
      "`docs/lidar_methodology_brief.md`. Paths are repo-relative.\n")
    w("Contents: 1 source tile index | 2 raster profiles | 3 checkpoints | 4 annotations | "
      "5 wells | 6 fold assignment | 7 result files verbatim | 8 run order | 9 full source code\n")

    idx = pd.read_csv(ROOT / "data/_source/lidar/laz_tile_index.csv")
    w("## 1. Source lidar tile index\nSource `data/_source/lidar/laz_tile_index.csv` "
      f"({len(idx)} rows, WesternPA D20 holdings). Raw LAZ is offloaded, not on this disk. Tiles overlapping each study tile:\n")
    for nm, b in (("9t", B9T), ("613590", B613)):
        m = idx[(idx.min_x < b[2]) & (idx.max_x > b[0]) & (idx.min_y < b[3]) & (idx.max_y > b[1])]
        w(f"### {nm}, bounds {b}, {len(m)} tiles\n")
        w(m.to_markdown(index=False))
        w("")

    w("## 2. Raster profiles\n")
    for r in RASTERS:
        p = ROOT / r
        try:
            w(f"- `{r}`: {prof(p)}" if p.exists() else f"- `{r}`: not on disk")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            w(f"- `{r}`: unreadable ({e})")

    w("\n## 3. Checkpoints (contents)\nmu and sd travel inside each checkpoint and are used at inference.\n")
    try:
        import torch
        for c in CKPTS:
            p = ROOT / c
            if not p.exists():
                w(f"- `{c}`: not on disk")
                continue
            ck = torch.load(p, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
            nparam = sum(v.numel() for v in ck["state_dict"].values()) if "state_dict" in ck else None
            small = {k: (v if np.size(v) < 20 else f"array{np.shape(v)}") for k, v in ck.items() if k != "state_dict"}
            w(f"- `{c}`: params {nparam}, keys {list(ck.keys())}\n  - {small}")
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        w(f"checkpoint read failed: {e}")

    w("\n## 4. Annotations (`qgis/annotations/annotations_proj.gpkg`)\n")
    for lay in ["pit_inside", "pit_outside", "pit_wall", "plat", "roads", "not_roads", "drainage"]:
        d = gpd.read_file(ANN, layer=lay)
        line = d.geom_type.iloc[0] == "LineString"
        a = d.geometry.length if line else d.geometry.area
        c9 = len(d.cx[B9T[0]:B9T[2], B9T[1]:B9T[3]])
        c6 = len(d.cx[B613[0]:B613[2], B613[1]:B613[3]])
        w(f"- `{lay}`: {len(d)} features, crs {d.crs.to_epsg()}, geom {sorted(d.geom_type.unique())}, columns {list(d.columns)}, "
          f"bounds {[round(x) for x in d.total_bounds]}, in 9t {c9}, in 613590 {c6}, "
          f"{'m length' if line else 'm2'} median {a.median():.1f} (p10 {a.quantile(.1):.1f}, p90 {a.quantile(.9):.1f})")

    w("\n## 5. Well records\n")
    for f in ["venango_wells_all.gpkg", "wells_in_tile.gpkg", "dep_wells_snapped_9tile.gpkg"]:
        p = ROOT / "data/_source/reference/dep_wells" / f
        if p.exists():
            d = gpd.read_file(p)
            w(f"- `data/_source/reference/dep_wells/{f}`: {len(d)} rows, crs {d.crs}, columns {list(d.columns)}")

    w("\n## 6. Fold assignment (block-wise 5-fold)\n")
    for nm, f in (("pit", "data/9t/models/pit/unet_cv5/pit_cv5_fold_assignment_9t.csv"),
                  ("pad", "data/9t/models/pad/unet_cv5/pad_cv5_fold_assignment_9t.csv")):
        p = ROOT / f
        if p.exists():
            d = pd.read_csv(p)
            w(f"### {nm}, `{f}`, columns {list(d.columns)}, {len(d)} rows\n")
            bc = "block_id" if "block_id" in d else d.columns[1]
            w(d.groupby("fold").agg(n=(d.columns[0], "size"), blocks=(bc, "nunique")).reset_index().to_markdown(index=False))
            w("")

    w("## 7. Result files (verbatim)\n")
    for f in VERBATIM:
        p = ROOT / f
        if p.exists():
            w(f"### {f}\n```{p.suffix.lstrip('.')}\n{p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')}\n```")
        else:
            w(f"### {f}\nnot found")

    w("## 8. Run order\n```")
    L.extend(RUN)
    w("```\nEnvironment: Python 3.13, PyTorch (CUDA, AMP), rasterio/GDAL, geopandas, shapely, scipy, numpy, pandas, "
      "matplotlib, PDAL 2.10 CLI (subprocess), WhiteboxTools, python-pptx, PowerPoint COM (render check).\n")

    w("## 9. Full source code\n")
    for s in SCRIPTS:
        p = ROOT / s
        w(f"### {s}\n```python\n{p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')}\n```" if p.exists() else f"### {s}\nnot found")
    OUT.write_text("\n".join(str(x) for x in L), encoding="utf-8")
    print("wrote", OUT, f"{OUT.stat().st_size / 1e6:.2f} MB")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
